vanilla_plus_preset_button_effect = {
	potential = { exists = from from = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00 } }
	allow = { always = yes custom_tooltip = { fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game" NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } } }
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			giga_preset_vanilla_plus = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = "giga_vanilla_plus_preset_button_tooltip"
	}
}

giga_menu_reset = {
	potential = { exists = from }
	allow = { hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } } }
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			switch = {
				trigger = has_global_flag
				giga_vanilla_plus_preset_selected = {
					giga_preset_vanilla_plus = yes
				}
				giga_gigas_experience_preset_selected = {
					giga_preset_gigas_experience = yes
				}
				giga_arcade_preset_selected = {
					giga_preset_arcade = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

giga_page_popout_preset = {
	potential = { 
		exists = from 
		from = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00 
				has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01
				has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02
				has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03
			} 
		} 
	}
	allow = { always = yes }
	effect = {}
}

giga_menu_prev = {
	potential = { exists = from }
	allow = { always = yes }
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			from = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

giga_menu_next = {
	potential = { exists = from }
	allow = { always = yes }
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			from = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_presets_02
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_02
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03 }
					remove_country_flag = giga_menu_page_03
					set_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# FE Stuff
giga_fe_megas_disabled = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
			has_global_flag = giga_fe_megas_disabled
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_fe_megas_disabled"
		hidden_effect = { remove_global_flag = giga_fe_megas_disabled }
	}
}

giga_fe_megas_enabled = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
			NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_fe_megas_disabled }
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_fe_megas_enabled"
		hidden_effect = {
			set_global_flag = giga_fe_megas_disabled
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_fe_gamers_disabled } }
				set_global_flag = giga_fe_gamers_disabled
			}
		}
	}
}

			NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_fe_planetcrafts_4 }
		}
	}
	allow = {

			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started }
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_fe_planetcrafts_4"
		remove_flags_fe_planetcrafts = yes
		set_global_flag = giga_fe_planetcrafts_4
	}

bulk_matter_x = { #BULK MATTER ENABLED
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
			has_global_flag = giga_bulk_matter_cost_disabled
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_bulk_matter_cost_disabled"
		hidden_effect = { remove_global_flag = giga_bulk_matter_cost_disabled  }
	}
}

	}

			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = giga_wrecked_ships_disabled
				has_global_flag = giga_wrecked_ships_juggs_disabled
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}

		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_disabled_juggs"
		hidden_effect = {

		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_divide_2_juggs = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_divide_2_juggs"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				divide_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
					value = 2
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_subtract_1_juggs = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "giga_ruined_ship_perc_low_enough_juggs"
			hidden_trigger = { 
				NOT = {
					event_target:global_event_country = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
							value < 10
						}
					}
				} 
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_subtract_1_juggs"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				subtract_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
					value = 10
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_reset_juggs = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_reset_juggs"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				set_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
					value = 100
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_add_1_juggs = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = { 
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game" 
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "giga_ruined_ship_perc_high_enough"
			hidden_trigger = { 
				NOT = {
					event_target:global_event_country = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
							value >= 99999
						}
					}
				} 
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_add_1_juggs"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				change_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
					value = 10
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
							value > 99999
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
						value = 99999
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_multiply_2_juggs = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "giga_ruined_ship_perc_high_enough"
			hidden_trigger = { 
				NOT = {
					event_target:global_event_country = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
							value >= 99999
						}
					}
				} 
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_multiply_2_juggs"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				multiply_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
					value = 2
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
							value > 99999
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						which = giga_ruined_warships_perc_juggs
						value = 99999
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "giga_ruined_ship_perc_low_enough"
			hidden_trigger = { 
				NOT = {
					event_target:global_event_country = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
							value < 10
						}
					}
				} 
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_subtract_1"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				subtract_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
					value = 10
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_reset = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {

		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = "must_not_have_started_game"
		hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_reset"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				set_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
					value = 100
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc
			}
		}
	}
}

ruined_ships_add_1 = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = { 
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game" 
			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "giga_ruined_ship_perc_high_enough"
			hidden_trigger = { 
				NOT = {
					event_target:global_event_country = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
							value >= 10000
						}
					}
				} 
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_ruined_ships_add_1"
		hidden_effect = { 
			event_target:global_event_country = {
				change_variable = {
					which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
					value = 10
				}
				ceiling_variable = giga_ruined_warships_perc
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
							value > 10000
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						which = giga_ruined_warships_perc
						value = 10000
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

							value > 10000
						}
					}
					set_variable = {

						value = 10000
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

#Vintage or not
katzenartig_x_serious = {
	potential = {
		exists = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_00
			NOR = {
				has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled
				has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled_cattail
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "must_not_have_started_game"
			NOT = { has_global_flag = giga_game_started }
		}
		hidden_trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled
				has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled_cattail
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = "giga_flusion_serious"
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled_cattail }
			remove_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled_cattail
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled }
			remove_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled 
		}
		set_global_flag = silly_flusionian_portraits_enabled 
	}
}

giga_vanilla_dyson_swarm_replace_enable = { 
	potential = { 
		exists = from 
		from = { 
			has_country_flag = giga_menu_page_01 
			NOT = { has_machine_age_dlc = no }
		} 
		NOT = { 
			has_global_flag = vanilla_dyson_swarm_replace_disabled 
		} 
	}																			
	allow = { always = yes }																																													
	effect = { 
		custom_tooltip = "giga_dyson_swarm_replace_enabled" 
		set_global_flag = vanilla_dyson_swarm_replace_disabled 
	} 
}

	potential = { 
		exists = from 
		from = {

			NOT = { has_machine_age_dlc = no }
		} 
		has_global_flag = vanilla_dyson_swarm_replace_disabled	
	}
																		
	allow = { always = yes }																																					
	effect = { 
		custom_tooltip = "giga_dyson_swarm_replace_disabled" 
		remove_global_flag = vanilla_dyson_swarm_replace_disabled 
	} 
}
